$ rp project review close --help
Records the decision "Already handled" and a done receipt carrying --note, in one step: the
card leaves the queue and the review-implement run never picks it up. If the issue is seen
again later, the publisher reopens it as a regression of that fix. Repeating the same close is safe.

Usage:
  rp project review close <card-id> [flags]

Flags:
      --note string   where the issue was handled (required)

Global Flags:
      --audience string   owner|technical (default owner; show/decide/close try owner, then technical)
      --no-preview        write large output artifacts but omit head/tail previews
      --out-dir string    directory for large output artifacts (default: RC_OUTPUT_DIR or .replypen/output)
  -o, --output string     output format: json|table (default: auto-detect)
      --profile string    token profile to use (default: RC_PROFILE, else auto — the brain in the current directory, else "default")
      --project string    scope the request to one project by name or id (default: RC_PROJECT; requires an all-projects token, unless the checkout's .replypen.toml binds that project via [[also]])
      --raw-output        disable progressive output spill and print full payloads to stdout
      --scope string      force request routing: project|tenant. "project" clears any resolved tenant (--tenant, a brain checkout, or a tenant-bound login) so a tenant-capable command hits the project route; "tenant" requires a resolvable tenant. With a tenant-pinned token, --scope project routes to the project and the server returns 403 — the flag controls routing, not authorization.
      --tenant string     override the login tenant where supported (default: RC_TENANT)

$ rp dev brain render --help
Show a tenant's compiled brain projection.

Every returned file is also written verbatim to .replypen/output/brain-render-<tenant>/tree/<brain-relative path>, so you can open the rendered playbooks/AGENTS.md instead of an opaque content.txt.

Artifacts are keyed by TENANT, not by sha: every run overwrites .replypen/output/brain-render-<tenant>/ (tree/ is wiped first, so a file from an earlier render never survives). To compare two shas/channels, move the first render aside before the second.

Usage:
  rp dev brain render --tenant <slug> [--path AGENTS.md] [--all] [--sha <commit> | --channel stable|edge] [flags]

Flags:
      --all                render every templated file (ignores --path)
      --channel string     managed channel to compile (stable or edge)
      --path stringArray   brain-relative path or glob to render (repeatable) (default [AGENTS.md])
      --sha string         exact full 40-character commit SHA to compile (default: the tenant's resolved channel)

Global Flags:
      --no-preview       write large output artifacts but omit head/tail previews
      --out-dir string   directory for large output artifacts (default: RC_OUTPUT_DIR or .replypen/output)
  -o, --output string    output format: json|table (default: auto-detect)
      --profile string   token profile to use (default: RC_PROFILE, else auto — the brain in the current directory, else "default")
      --project string   scope the request to one project by name or id (default: RC_PROJECT; requires an all-projects token, unless the checkout's .replypen.toml binds that project via [[also]])
      --raw-output       disable progressive output spill and print full payloads to stdout
      --scope string     force request routing: project|tenant. "project" clears any resolved tenant (--tenant, a brain checkout, or a tenant-bound login) so a tenant-capable command hits the project route; "tenant" requires a resolvable tenant. With a tenant-pinned token, --scope project routes to the project and the server returns 403 — the flag controls routing, not authorization.
      --tenant string    override the login tenant where supported (default: RC_TENANT)

"project" clears any resolved tenant (--tenant, a brain checkout, or a tenant-bound login) so a tenant-capable command hits the project route; "tenant" requires a resolvable tenant. With a tenant-pinned token, --scope project routes to the project and the server returns 403 — the flag controls routing, not authorization.
      --tenant string    override the login tenant where supported (default: RC_TENANT)

$ rp dev brain promote --help
Promote an exact tested commit to a project brain channel

Usage:
  rp dev brain promote --channel stable|edge --sha <commit> [flags]

Flags:
      --channel string   managed channel to move (stable or edge)
      --sha string       exact full 40-character commit SHA

Global Flags:
      --no-preview       write large output artifacts but omit head/tail previews
      --out-dir string   directory for large output artifacts (default: RC_OUTPUT_DIR or .replypen/output)
  -o, --output string    output format: json|table (default: auto-detect)
      --profile string   token profile to use (default: RC_PROFILE, else auto — the brain in the current directory, else "default")
      --project string   scope the request to one project by name or id (default: RC_PROJECT; requires an all-projects token, unless the checkout's .replypen.toml binds that project via [[also]])
      --raw-output       disable progressive output spill and print full payloads to stdout
      --scope string     force request routing: project|tenant. "project" clears any resolved tenant (--tenant, a brain checkout, or a tenant-bound login) so a tenant-capable command hits the project route; "tenant" requires a resolvable tenant. $ rp dev console database query --help
Run a guarded production database query

Usage:
  rp dev console database query <db> <sql|-> [flags]

Flags:
      --all                     stream every row from one repeatable-read database snapshot
      --allow-truncated         accept an inline result that the server truncated
      --dry-run                 execute on the write plane, report rows affected + RETURNING rows, then ROLL BACK; requires --write
      --format string           query data format: json|ndjson|csv|tsv
      --limit int               max inline rows, or --all stream batch size (defaults: 500 inline, 5000 all; max 5000)
      --out string              write data to PATH; - writes stdout; auto writes a unique .replypen/output artifact
      --param stringArray       bind @k in SQL as text using k=v (repeatable)
      --principal-id string     principal external id (the asserted identity); requires --principal-kind
      --principal-kind string   bind the call to a principal kind (e.g. kampadmin_admin); requires --principal-id
      --write                   execute against the project's sealed write-plane DSN (<X>_WRITE_DSN in .env.action) and COMMIT; requires scope console:db:write

Global Flags:
      --no-preview       write large output artifacts but omit head/tail previews
      --out-dir string   directory for large output artifacts (default: RC_OUTPUT_DIR or .replypen/output)
  -o, --output string    output format: json|table (default: auto-detect)
      --profile string   token profile to use (default: RC_PROFILE, else auto — the brain in the current directory, else "default")
      --project string   scope the request to one project by name or id (default: RC_PROJECT; requires an all-projects token, unless the checkout's .replypen.toml binds that project via [[also]])
      --raw-output       disable progressive output spill and print full payloads to stdout
      --scope string     force request routing: project|tenant. "project" clears any resolved tenant (--tenant, a brain checkout, or a tenant-bound login) so a tenant-capable command hits the project route; "tenant" requires a resolvable tenant. With a tenant-pinned token, --scope project routes to the project and the server returns 403 — the flag controls routing, not authorization.
      --tenant string    override the login tenant where supported (default: RC_TENANT)

$ rp dev console paths --help
Reports dir/file/missing for each absolute path as a real production workspace sees it
(/brain, /tenant, /mirrors/<repo>, /kb/<provider>, …), optionally per tenant. Existence only:
surface-scoped docs are stubbed in the console, so file content there is not representative,
and /skills is not mounted in the console at all. A single - reads newline-separated paths from
stdin. Exits 4 when any path is missing or any checked tenant failed. Scripts reading -o json
should add --raw-output so a large report is never replaced by a spill manifest.

Usage:
  rp dev console paths [--tenants a,b|all] <path>...|- [flags]

Flags:
      --concurrency int   max tenants checked in parallel (default 4)
      --tenants string    check each listed tenant (comma-separated), or all active tenants of the project

Global Flags:
      --no-preview       write large output artifacts but omit head/tail previews
      --out-dir string   directory for large output artifacts (default: RC_OUTPUT_DIR or .replypen/output)
  -o, --output string    output format: json|table (default: auto-detect)
      --profile string   token profile to use (default: RC_PROFILE, else auto — the brain in the current directory, else "default")
      --project string   scope the request to one project by name or id (default: RC_PROJECT; requires an all-projects token, unless the checkout's .replypen.toml binds that project via [[also]])
      --raw-output       disable progressive output spill and print full payloads to stdout
      --scope string     force request routing: project|tenant. "project" clears any resolved tenant (--tenant, a brain checkout, or a tenant-bound login) so a tenant-capable command hits the project route; "tenant" requires a resolvable tenant. With a tenant-pinned token, --scope project routes to the project and the server returns 403 — the flag controls routing, not authorization.
      --tenant string    override the login tenant where supported (default: RC_TENANT)

$ rp dev console file get --help
Download a file or directory from the console sandbox, any size (up to 512 MiB), byte-exact.
Directories arrive as a local directory (tar'ed in the sandbox, unpacked here). Paths outside
/tmp and /home/agent (e.g. /kb, /brain, /mirrors) are staged through /tmp transparently.
local-path defaults to the sandbox path's base name in the current directory; - writes a file to stdout.

Usage:
  rp dev console file get <sandbox-path> [local-path] [flags]

Aliases:
  get, download

Examples:
  rp dev console file get /kb/knowledgeowl ./kb
  rp dev console file get /tmp/rc-console/stdout-7.txt ./stdout.txt

Flags:
      --out string              local destination path, or - for stdout (same as the local-path argument)
      --principal-id string     principal external id (the asserted identity); requires --principal-kind
      --principal-kind string   bind the call to a principal kind (e.g. kampadmin_admin); requires --principal-id

Global Flags:
      --no-preview       write large output artifacts but omit head/tail previews
      --out-dir string   directory for large output artifacts (default: RC_OUTPUT_DIR or .replypen/output)
  -o, --output string    output format: json|table (default: auto-detect)
      --profile string   token profile to use (default: RC_PROFILE, else auto — the brain in the current directory, else "default")
      --project string   scope the request to one project by name or id (default: RC_PROJECT; requires an all-projects token, unless the checkout's .replypen.toml binds that project via [[also]])
      --raw-output       disable progressive output spill and print full payloads to stdout
      --scope string     force request routing: project|tenant. "project" clears any resolved tenant (--tenant, a brain checkout, or a tenant-bound login) so a tenant-capable command hits the project route; "tenant" requires a resolvable tenant. With a tenant-pinned token, --scope project routes to the project and the server returns 403 — the flag controls routing, not authorization.
      --tenant string    override the login tenant where supported (default: RC_TENANT)

$ rp dev console action preflight --help
The brain's Python actions/<id>/preflight.py runs first in the session workspace (composed grounding env, tenant-scoped DSNs) and reports {ok, summary, observed, resource_url}.
A refusal short-circuits: status is preflight_failed and no dry-run is attempted.
On Embassy projects a passing preflight is followed by the Embassy would_execute dry-run (digest/schema/tenant checked on the customer app); hosted projects stop after the preflight.
Nothing is executed either way.

Usage:
  rp dev console action preflight <id> --params '{...}' [flags]

Flags:
      --params string   JSON object of action params

Global Flags:
      --no-preview       write large output artifacts but omit head/tail previews
      --out-dir string   directory for large output artifacts (default: RC_OUTPUT_DIR or .replypen/output)
  -o, --output string    output format: json|table (default: auto-detect)
      --profile string   token profile to use (default: RC_PROFILE, else auto — the brain in the current directory, else "default")
      --project string   scope the request to one project by name or id (default: RC_PROJECT; requires an all-projects token, unless the checkout's .replypen.toml binds that project via [[also]])
      --raw-output       disable progressive output spill and print full payloads to stdout
      --scope string     force request routing: project|tenant. "project" clears any resolved tenant (--tenant, a brain checkout, or a tenant-bound login) so a tenant-capable command hits the project route; "tenant" requires a resolvable tenant. With a tenant-pinned token, --scope project routes to the project and the server returns 403 — the flag controls routing, not authorization.
      --tenant string    override the login tenant where supported (default: RC_TENANT)

With a tenant-pinned token, --scope project routes to the project and the server returns 403 — the flag controls routing, not authorization.
      --tenant string    override the login tenant where supported (default: RC_TENANT)

Use "rp dev tools provider [command] --help" for more information about a command.

      --out-dir string   directory for large output artifacts (default: RC_OUTPUT_DIR or .replypen/output)
  -o, --output string    output format: json|table (default: auto-detect)
      --profile string   token profile to use (default: RC_PROFILE, else auto — the brain in the current directory, else "default")
      --project string   scope the request to one project by name or id (default: RC_PROJECT; requires an all-projects token, unless the checkout's .replypen.toml binds that project via [[also]])
      --raw-output       disable progressive output spill and print full payloads to stdout
      --scope string     force request routing: project|tenant. $ rp dev onboarding goto --help
Re-seeds the world's project (every earlier step confirmed, pulls settled from fixtures) on the
make dev server and prints its URL. Worlds: northwind (default), website-failed, website-unreachable,
website-partial, personal, empty-inbox, few-sent, sorting-failed. A named preset (slow-analysis,
inbox-reading, website-reading, draft-loading, …) owns its step: no --step, the server sets its holds
and clock first. The server lists both sets on an unknown world. The dev url comes from --url, else
REPLYPEN_BASE_URL.

Usage:
  rp dev onboarding goto [flags]

Examples:
  rp dev onboarding goto --step 4
  rp dev onboarding goto --step email_types --world sorting-failed --url http://localhost:24729
  rp dev onboarding goto --world slow-analysis

Flags:

      --url string     make dev url (default REPLYPEN_BASE_URL)
      --world string   fixture world (default "northwind")

Global Flags:

      --out-dir string   directory for large output artifacts (default: RC_OUTPUT_DIR or .replypen/output)
  -o, --output string    output format: json|table (default: auto-detect)

$ rp dev platform-skills pull --help
Fetches the manifest of the platform skill library the host mounts at /skills/<name> in runs and,
when its digest is not cached yet, downloads and verifies the archive into <cache>/<digest>/.
The brain kit's docker gate (brain_test.py / brain_run.py --mode docker) mounts that directory.
--offline prints the last pulled snapshot without contacting the host.

Usage:

  rp dev platform-skills pull -o json

$ rp fleet runs --help
Page GET /api/v1/runs and render the fleet digest: a per-run line with health flags + turns, the aggregate rates, and the worst-offender shortlists. --format agent gives the token-lean computed digest for an agent to triage; passing --format explicitly renders the digest even when stdout is piped. --all fans out across every project (all-projects token), grouped per project with a fleet total. -o json is a raw passthrough of the paged run rows (keyed by project under --all) and takes precedence over --format.

Usage:
  rp fleet runs [flags]

Flags:
      --all                       fan out across every project (requires an all-projects token)
      --days int                  window label for the digest header (days) (default 7)
      --format string             output style: human|agent (default "human")
      --from string               sender address or name substring (case-insensitive)
      --kind string               filter by kind: email|prompt|mcp|analysis|console|chat
      --learning string[="any"]   filter by learning signal; bare means any, or use =feedback|sent_delta|triage_skipped|triage_corrected
      --limit int                 max runs to include (1..100, uncapped by default)
      --reviewed                  only runs with a 1–5 human review score (includes held-out eval runs)
      --since string              runs created at/after this RFC3339 time or YYYY-MM-DD (UTC); overrides --days
      --subject string            thread subject substring (case-insensitive)
      --timeline                  add the per-day runs/errors/latency timeline
      --until string              runs created before this RFC3339 time, or through this YYYY-MM-DD (UTC)

Global Flags:
      --no-preview       write large output artifacts but omit head/tail previews
      --out-dir string   directory for large output artifacts (default: RC_OUTPUT_DIR or .replypen/output)
  -o, --output string    output format: json|table (default: auto-detect)
      --profile string   token profile to use (default: RC_PROFILE, else auto — the brain in the current directory, else "default")
      --project string   scope the request to one project by name or id (default: RC_PROJECT; requires an all-projects token, unless the checkout's .replypen.toml binds that project via [[also]])
      --raw-output       disable progressive output spill and print full payloads to stdout
      --scope string     force request routing: project|tenant. "project" clears any resolved tenant (--tenant, a brain checkout, or a tenant-bound login) so a tenant-capable command hits the project route; "tenant" requires a resolvable tenant. With a tenant-pinned token, --scope project routes to the project and the server returns 403 — the flag controls routing, not authorization.
      --tenant string    override the login tenant where supported (default: RC_TENANT)

$ rp fleet actions --help
Find operator-visible actions across recent runs without downloading the fleet event feed. Repeat --action and --status to OR exact values. Results are cursor-paged automatically.       --status stringArray   filter by status: proposed|executing|succeeded|failed|canceled (repeatable; OR)

Global Flags:

$ rp fleet health --help
Fetch GET /api/v1/health and render the healthy/unhealthy sections (stale/failing mirrors, failed brain boot checks, dead-lettered runs). Exits non-zero when unhealthy, so it's usable in CI/cron. --all fans out across every project (all-projects token) and exits non-zero if ANY project is unhealthy; an all-projects token with no --project falls back to the fan-out automatically. -o json passes the raw server rows through; the exit code still reflects the verdict.

Usage:

      --all         fan out across every project (requires an all-projects token)

      --raw-output       disable progressive output spill and print full payloads to stdout

$ rp fleet deploy-state --help
Fetch GET /api/v1/deploy-state and render what is LIVE per plane: the host release the server was built from, the project brain's channel pointers (plus the recorded promotion timeline), and each source mirror's checked-out commit (plus its refresh timeline). --host-repo <path> additionally lists the commits on that local ReplyPen checkout's main that the deployed release does not contain (offline; it never fetches). -o json passes the raw server rows through, with the local delta added under "unpromoted".

Page GET /api/v1/run-events, /api/v1/egress-log, and /api/v1/api-log and cluster them like run_patterns: bash-failure signatures, blocked-egress hosts, allowed endpoint use, and abnormal write volume, with suggested-fix stub. human and agent formats render the same clustered view; passing --format explicitly renders it even when stdout is piped. --all fans out across every project (all-projects token), one clustered section per project. -o json is a raw passthrough of all three paged feeds (keyed by project under --all) and takes precedence over --format.

      --out-dir string   directory for large output artifacts (default: RC_OUTPUT_DIR or .replypen/output)
  -o, --output string    output format: json|table (default: auto-detect)
      --profile string   token profile to use (default: RC_PROFILE, else auto — the brain in the current directory, else "default")
      --project string   scope the request to one project by name or id (default: RC_PROJECT; requires an all-projects token, unless the checkout's .replypen.toml binds that project via [[also]])
      --raw-output       disable progressive output spill and print full payloads to stdout
      --scope string     force request routing: project|tenant. "project" clears any resolved tenant (--tenant, a brain checkout, or a tenant-bound login) so a tenant-capable command hits the project route; "tenant" requires a resolvable tenant. With a tenant-pinned token, --scope project routes to the project and the server returns 403 — the flag controls routing, not authorization.
      --tenant string    override the login tenant where supported (default: RC_TENANT)

$ rp auth login --help
Sign in to ReplyPen. By default this opens your browser and catches the redirect on a
localhost port (PKCE). On a headless/SSH box, use --device to get a short code you approve in
a browser on any device.

Tokens are stored per profile in /tmp/replypen-cli-help-test/replypen/tokens.json (0600). Pick the project (or
all-projects, if you're an admin) on the browser consent screen.

Usage:
  rp auth login [flags]

Flags:
      --device   use the device-authorization flow (headless/SSH — no local browser)

Global Flags:
      --no-preview       write large output artifacts but omit head/tail previews
      --out-dir string   directory for large output artifacts (default: RC_OUTPUT_DIR or .replypen/output)
  -o, --output string    output format: json|table (default: auto-detect)

$ rp self update --help
Update rp to the latest GitHub release for this OS/arch.

On Linux/WSL/Windows this replaces one unambiguous running binary in place. On macOS it
updates the canonical Homebrew cask; use --migrate to canonicalize a mixed installation.

Usage:
  rp self update [flags]

Flags:
      --check     only report whether a newer version exists; install nothing
      --migrate   on macOS, canonicalize onto Homebrew and remove verified legacy Go installs

Global Flags:
      --no-preview       write large output artifacts but omit head/tail previews
      --out-dir string   directory for large output artifacts (default: RC_OUTPUT_DIR or .replypen/output)
  -o, --output string    output format: json|table (default: auto-detect)
